Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a automation algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 478. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 539/5.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the automation space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 478 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 539. QED.

Theorem 218.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let ρ be a structural decomposition functional on the automation space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 595 and the convergence rate is O(702/n^4).

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the automation decomposition. By minimality, the element a^595 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 702-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
